- **louvain**: How to use Louvain, the person's permissioned company memory, well. Use when the person asks about their own work (colleagues, customers, deals, projects, decisions, commitments, meetings, mail or documents inside their company) and the Louvain tools are connected, and whenever you read a Louvain answer, cite one, or save something to Louvain. ([louvain-ai/claude-plugin](https://github.com/louvain-ai/claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/louvain))
- **louvain-benchmark-comparison**: Use when you must quantify whether cell-attribute aware community detection (EVA/ELVAR) attains stronger significance for a known simulated or real DA shift than standard Louvain clustering on the same single-cell graph. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/louvain-benchmark-comparison))
- **louvain-cell-type-clustering**: Use when training scDesign2 in scReadSim on a gene-by-cell UMI count matrix whose cells lack predefined cell labels (no celllabel_file), since scDesign2 requires discrete cell types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/louvain-cell-type-clustering))
- **louvain-centroid-initialization**: Use when training a graph autoencoder for scRNA-seq cell clustering (e.g. scGAE) and initial cluster centers are needed before soft-assignment/KL clustering training; also relevant to any DEC-style pipeline requiring centroid initialization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/louvain-centroid-initialization))
- **louvain-clustering-benchmarking**: Use when a simulated or ground-truth-labelled scRNA-Seq dataset is available and you need to quantify whether cell-attribute aware EVA community detection recovers known labels better than a Louvain-analog, via ARI and chi-square DA statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/louvain-clustering-benchmarking))
- **louvain-clustering-bio-conservation-scoring**: Use when comparing the output embeddings of multiple scRNA-seq integration methods (e.g., scDREAMER, scVI, Harmony) and you need standardized, composite bio-conservation and batch-correction scores to rank them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/louvain-clustering-bio-conservation-scoring))
- **louvain-clustering-nmi-optimization**: Use when evaluating integrated scRNA-seq embeddings (e.g., scDREAMER vs. scVI/Scanorama/Harmony baselines) and you need per-method cluster assignments that are resolution-optimized before computing NMI, ARI, or ASW metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/louvain-clustering-nmi-optimization))
- **louvain-clustering-of-control-cells**: Use when a perturbation scRNAseq analysis requires reference cell-state labels from the control condition alone (scanpy neighbors + Louvain on control cells) before NCFS feature weighting and semi-supervised clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/louvain-clustering-of-control-cells))
- **louvain-clustering-of-embeddings**: Use when a cell-level embedding matrix (e.g., scGAE latent representation of Splatter-simulated scenario3/4 scRNA-seq data) is available and discrete cell clusters must be recovered and scored by NMI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/louvain-clustering-of-embeddings))
- **louvain-clustering-of-simulated-trajectories**: Use when an ensemble of simulated differentiation trajectories (from RNA-velocity Markov sampling) must be partitioned into sub-trajectory groups within each terminal state before DTW alignment and consensus construction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/louvain-clustering-of-simulated-trajectories))
- **louvain-community-clustering**: Use when a scRNA-seq matrix (cells × features/PCs) has been reduced to a Sphetcher spherical sketch (indicator vector with sketch_size ones) and the sketched cells must be clustered into cell types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/louvain-community-clustering))
- **louvain-community-clustering-single-cell**: Use when single-cell AnnData carries per-cell training-dynamics scores ('conf', 'var') from Annotatability and the goal is to cluster cells by annotation-trainability signal, e.g. recovering disease/intermediate/healthy-like β cell states and comparing treatment subgroups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/louvain-community-clustering-single-cell))
- **louvain-community-detection**: Use when a weighted metabolite similarity network (e.g. GNPS/MassIVE cosine-similarity edges) must be partitioned into communities without preset cluster counts, to reconstruct MCN structure or quantify singleton reduction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/louvain-community-detection))
- **louvain-community-detection-on-cell-graphs**: Use when a QC-filtered, normalized scRNA-seq object already carries variable features and PCA coordinates (e.g. PBMC3k with 25 PCs from 10-fold SVD cross-validation) and cell-population cluster labels are needed before marker-gene testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/louvain-community-detection-on-cell-graphs))
- **louvain-community-detection-on-gene-regulatory-networks**: Use when you have a filtered differential GRN edge list (e.g., BoostDiff output post filter_network) and need to partition it into gene modules for pathway enrichment (gseapy Enrichr KEGG) or module-level interpretation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/louvain-community-detection-on-gene-regulatory-networks))
- **louvain-community-detection-on-grn**: Use when you have a weighted gene network (e.g. from scPRINT attention-based inference) and want to identify coherent gene modules for pathway/gene-set enrichment, as done for BPH fibroblast ion-exchange communities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/louvain-community-detection-on-grn))
- **louvain-community-detection-on-molecular-networks**: Use when a scored NP Analyst network (nodes = MS features/samples, edges = bioactivity-weighted associations) needs partitioning into communities for Compound Activity Mapping visualization and prioritization of bioactive MS features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/louvain-community-detection-on-molecular-networks))
- **louvain-community-detection-on-single-cell-sketches**: Use when evaluating whether a cell-sampling/sketching strategy (e.g., spherical thresholding vs. geometric/box sketching) preserves biological cell-type separability in large scRNA-seq datasets, by clustering sketched subsets and scoring against known cell-type labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/louvain-community-detection-on-single-cell-sketches))
- **louvain-graph-based-cell-clustering**: Use when a Seurat object has been normalized (LogNormalize, scale factor 10,000) and PCA-reduced, and cell populations must be identified and later assigned labels (hepatocytes, endothelial cells, MAIT cells, GZMB+ GZMK+ NK cells, S100A12+ neutrophils). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/louvain-graph-based-cell-clustering))
- **louvain-graph-clustering**: Use when single-cell ATAC-seq cells need to be grouped into clusters (e.g., to produce per-cluster consensus CNV genotypes with Copy-scAT's smoothClusters, or to define subpopulations prior to downstream smoothing/filtering). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/louvain-graph-clustering))
- **louvain-graph-clustering**: Use when a spatial metabolomics peak table (pixels x ion intensities with X/Y coordinates) needs to be segmented into spatial tissue regions whose alignment with histology (e.g. H&E) must be assessed, or when comparing preprocessing impact on clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/louvain-graph-clustering))
- **louvain-graph-clustering**: Use when QC-passed, SCTransform-normalized and integrated single-cell expression data needs unbiased cell-cluster identification prior to marker-based cell-type annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/louvain-graph-clustering--c593852a6e23ee72))
- **louvain-graph-clustering**: Use when droplet-level counts (e.g., 10X sparse matrix from mouse brain snRNA-seq) have been filtered and converted to a Seurat object and you need data-driven clusters to detect residual debris/background droplets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/louvain-graph-clustering--254d98e3a8f6bdcf))
- **louvain-graph-clustering**: Use when a scRNA-seq clustering step must discover data-driven cell groups from a PCA/KNN graph — e.g. generating I-clusters prior to scCAD's cluster decomposition or splitting clusters larger than R*n cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/louvain-graph-clustering--ae6203732f5c17a9))
- **louvain-graph-clustering**: Use when clustering cells from a log1p-normalized AnnData object into graph communities — e.g., defining control cell states prior to NCFS gene weighting, running sslouvain with fixed control labels, or clustering integrated embeddings to test whether a method detects perturbation-unique responses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/louvain-graph-clustering--f66fc6cd6ccf9a63))
- **louvain-graph-clustering-of-gene-weights**: Use when you have cohort-shared ICA gene z-weight vectors for independent components and need to collapse them into consensus, reproducible gene programs (meta-components) for downstream TME scRNA-seq annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/louvain-graph-clustering-of-gene-weights))
- **louvain-graph-community-clustering**: Use when you have an all-pairs spectral similarity matrix (e.g., Spec2Vec or modified cosine over ~95,320 positive-ionization MS/MS spectra) and need to decompose the molecular network into well-connected communities for downstream structural clustering analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/louvain-graph-community-clustering))
- **louvain-latent-space-clustering**: Use when you have a MuData object with scConfluence latent embeddings in mdata.obsm['latent'] and need to discover subpopulations (e.g. memory/naive/plasma B cell subtypes in tri-omics PBMC data) that single-modality annotations miss. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/louvain-latent-space-clustering))
- **louvain-partition-consumption**: Use when a molecular network already has Louvain community labels (e.g., from a spectral-similarity edge list) and you need interpretable, cycle-free per-cluster subnetworks that preserve connectivity of every assigned metabolite. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/louvain-partition-consumption))
- **louvain-purity-modularity-alpha-tuning**: Use when running cell-attribute aware (EVA) community detection on a cell-cell nearest-neighbor graph and an optimal alpha must be chosen before attribute-aware clustering and downstream DA testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/louvain-purity-modularity-alpha-tuning))
- **louvain-resolution-parameter-sweep**: Use when comparing multimodal single-cell clustering methods on the same CITE-seq dataset and you need a fair resolution-matched comparison plus a resolution sweep to explain divergent subset recovery and time efficiency. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/louvain-resolution-parameter-sweep))
- **louvain-subpopulation-reclustering**: Use when a biologically annotated cell subpopulation needs finer subdivision, and a low-dimensional embedding from scGAE (20-D latent space of scRNA-seq counts) is available; specifically to confirm substructure such as ≥2 subclusters within Ba/Eo/MaP cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/louvain-subpopulation-reclustering))
- **louvain-two-group-iterative-reclustering**: Use when CrossTimeAnnotation has assigned labels to t1 scRNA-seq cells but some cells may represent newly emerged cell types that the t0-trained neural network classifier would mislabel. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/louvain-two-group-iterative-reclustering))
